[427] The continued strengthening of national defense reforms and the enhancement of defense capabilities, as well as the pursuit of security cooperation in the Asia-Pacific region and international support, are absolutely not intended to engage in an arms race with the CCP, nor are they attempts to provoke the other side. They are entirely based on the needs of national security and self-defense, and the responsibilities and obligations that must be undertaken to maintain peace in the Taiwan Strait and regional stability

[428] Faced with the CCP's unwavering commitment to invading Taiwan by force and its annual double-digit growth in defense budgets for military modernization, our national defense security faces severe challenges. Given that the other side currently has no intention of establishing a peaceful and stable framework with us, the National Armed Forces, based on their duty to protect the nation and its people, must continuously strengthen their self-defense capabilities and build a solid military force to protect themselves and serve as a powerful deterrent against aggression

[429] Part Two: National Defense Policy and Military Strategy 63 In view of the ever-evolving strategic environment and the rapid development of science and technology, the national armed forces' building and preparation work should be actively and continuously promoted

[430] We must continuously introduce new high-tech weapons and equipment to build a high-quality combat force, avoid an imbalance of military power between the two sides of the Taiwan Strait, strive for a qualitative advantage, defeat the enemy, deter the enemy from easily starting a war, and ensure the country's sustainable survival and development

[431] Develop defensive and countermeasure forces. In order to implement the strategic concept of "effective deterrence and defensive defense", the ROC Armed Forces will expand its strategic depth by deploying air and sea control missiles

[432] In order to adapt to future changes in the strategic environment and effectively maintain the military advantage in the Taiwan Strait, in addition to strengthening technological integration and performance, we will actively develop and acquire precision and long-range weapon systems and establish electronic countermeasures units to enhance our deep strike capabilities

[433] However, at the very beginning of an enemy's invasion of Taiwan, a tactical counterattack should be launched in a timely manner to gain the upper hand; and in the selection of targets, the focus should be on key military targets that pose the greatest threat to us, and counterattacks should never be carried out against civilian facilities or innocent civilians

[434] Given the increasingly diverse nature of future warfare, the procurement of weapons and equipment by the National Armed Forces aims to enhance the overall combat capability of joint operations among the three branches of the armed forces, and to build up superior combat capabilities in ground, air, sea, underwater, and cyber warfare, in order to deter enemy offensives and reduce the chances of a successful invasion of Taiwan

[435] In the future, if advanced weapon systems such as new submarines, anti-submarine patrol aircraft, and air defense missiles are acquired, and combined with the "Po Sheng Project" C4ISR system, it will be more conducive to integrating and maximizing the joint combat effectiveness, effectively deterring enemy attempts to invade Taiwan, and ensuring national security

[436] Territorial Defense Faced with the ever-increasing military threat and challenges from the CCP, the ROC Armed Forces' current strategic thinking focuses on "combat power" and "deployment" in order to defend national security

[437] The Ministry of National Defense will continue to build a reliable combat force of appropriate size, high efficiency, and rapid response in accordance with the "Ten Year Armed Forces Development Plan" and the "Streamlining Project" to promote the transformation of the national armed forces. It will also increase the flexibility of combat operations by adjusting defense deployments

[438] If war is unavoidable, the mission of territorial defense will be accomplished through the rapid, flexible, and efficient joint military power and the people's defense forces to ensure national security

[439] I) Combat Power The National Armed Forces will continue to plan in accordance with the future military building concept, implement the acquisition of weapon systems and the improvement of combat power at each stage --- Page 86 --- 64 National Defense Report of the Republic of China in 2004, and combine the National Armed Forces' "Streamlining Project" force structure adjustment to comprehensively promote military transformation in order to build a modern armed force that is flexible in organization, rapid in response, and highly effective

[440] Strengthen the joint combat capabilities of the three services. With the continuous enhancement of "joint operations" capabilities as the current main focus of military development, this will be achieved through concrete actions such as accelerating the construction of C4ISR systems and integrating weapon platform systems, improving joint operations command mechanisms, completing the revision of joint operations guidelines and related operational plans, and strengthening joint operations training of troops, thereby enhancing the joint operations effectiveness of the armed forces and achieving the goal of integrated operations and joint actions among the three services

[441] Promote the transformation of the armed forces. Combining the technological, information-based, and rapid characteristics of modern warfare, continuously review and innovate aspects such as force size, organizational structure, troop missions, strategic thinking, and guidelines training. At the same time, expand talent acquisition channels, improve the quality of national defense personnel, introduce modern management concepts and improve organizational management to establish a professional armed forces "based on knowledge" in order to effectively respond to the national defense challenges of the information age

[442] II) Deployment In accordance with the strategic concept of "effective deterrence and defensive defense", the defense deployment will be adjusted to implement "national defense for all people", strengthen joint combat capabilities and increase the flexibility of military operations

[443] When war is unavoidable, we must resolutely implement territorial defense, combine the defense forces of the entire population, give full play to the joint combat effectiveness of the three armed forces, adhere to the principle of "being able to fight in order to make peace, and being prepared for war without fear of war," and use all effective defensive countermeasures to achieve the goals of repelling, driving back, and annihilating the enemy, so as to safeguard territorial security and ensure the sustainable survival of the nation

[444] III. Counter-terrorism and Countermeasures: In the face of the increasingly serious international terrorist threat, as well as other major emergencies and crises concerning national security, and the unrestricted and asymmetric warfare that the CCP may wage against our country, "early warning" and "countermeasures" are important tools for effective prevention and rapid response

[445] Once an incident occurs, the government's national security decision-making system will coordinate and direct the response, with support from professional armed forces and other relevant government departments. They will assist the government in responding to the crisis, controlling the disaster, maintaining public order, and carrying out damage recovery work to quickly resolve the crisis

[446] a) Early warning: The prevention of national defense and security crises must be carried out in peacetime by grasping the development of crisis signs and obtaining early warnings in order to make crisis predictions and preventive measures, and prevent the outbreak of serious crisis situations

[447] The National Armed Forces will cooperate with the National Security Council's crisis early warning mechanism and the Executive Yuan's "Inter-Ministerial Crisis Response Task Force" mechanism, combining the sharing and exchange of crisis information among government agencies such as the procuratorate, police, investigation, intelligence, and law enforcement, to ensure the timeliness of crisis early warning and response, and to swiftly implement crisis prevention measures

[448] Countering Changes: The Armed Forces as a Foundation for the Nation As an important link in the crisis response system, in order to cope with future national security threats that exceed the norm, the Armed Forces will continue to improve its "emergency response, rapid response, and immediate combat" capabilities in accordance with the principles of "organization, efficiency, directness, and speed," strengthen the functions of crisis management mechanisms, and enhance the Armed Forces' professional capabilities in counter-terrorism and change response, so as to safeguard the security of the nation and its people

[449] Improve crisis response mechanisms and capabilities. In accordance with the tasks assigned by the National Security Council’s “Government Response Mechanism for Major Domestic Emergencies (Sudden Incidents),” improve the Ministry of National Defense’s crisis response capabilities and formulate the “Operational Plan for Coordinating with the National Crisis Prevention and Response Mechanism.” With the Armed Forces’ “Joint Command Mechanism” as the core, assign the duty roster system the task of “Crisis Response Center.” Continuously review the organization of tasks and execution processes to comprehensively enhance the Armed Forces’ response and response capabilities, so as to effectively support the government in carrying out major national security crisis management

[450] Enhance the professional capabilities of the armed forces in counterterrorism and counter-insurgency. Based on functionality, select key support targets, clearly define the responsibility areas for "counterterrorism operations", and organize the armed forces into dedicated counterterrorism units to support the government's counterterrorism operations

[451] In addition, we continue to procure various advanced counter-terrorism equipment to enhance the effectiveness of the National Armed Forces' counter-terrorism and counter-terrorism operations. Through the National Armed Forces' various intelligence, surveillance, and reconnaissance systems and security systems, we continue to exchange intelligence with the prosecution, police, investigation, intelligence, and governance agencies. We combine national security, police administration, public mobilization, fire services, health, environmental protection, and civilian rescue systems to achieve integrated operations and complete the counter-terrorism and counter-terrorism mission

[452] Republic of China 2004 National Defense Report, Section 5: National Defense Policy Under the guidance of the three basic national defense policy objectives of "preventing war," "national defense," and "counter-terrorism and crisis management," and facing the significant expansion of the Chinese Communist Party's military power, the shocking threat of international terrorist activities, and the repeated tests of major domestic disasters, in order to safeguard the interests of all citizens and ensure overall national security, our current important national defense policy is to pragmatically promote various national defense affairs through strengthening the national defense system, integrating traditional combat capabilities, and training elite troops. We are also actively adjusting the priorities of policy implementation, focusing on "strengthening the overall national defense concept," "improving crisis management and response mechanisms," and "building a high-quality and efficient system." "Highly modern military" and other aspects

[453] The specific policy guidelines are as follows: I. Expanding Public Participation in National Defense To promote public understanding and recognition of national defense, national defense policy will aim for efficient administration and a clean, efficient, and convenient work performance. Through the transparency and openness of national defense information, all sectors of society can participate in the discussion of national defense affairs. By using diverse information channels, we will strengthen the promotion of relevant national defense policies to build public consensus and enhance public confidence. At the same time, we will continue to promote national defense mobilization and preparation, integrate national human, material, and financial resources, deepen public awareness of national defense, enhance the public's sense of crisis, and strive for public support and participation in national defense

Strengthening the Concept of Overall National Defense To prevent social panic and economic crises that may be caused by various emergencies, affecting national security, This concept of total national defense, in accordance with the "National Defense Mobilization and Preparation Act," actively engages in mobilization and preparation in education, spirit, human resources, material resources, financial resources, transportation, health, science and technology, and military affairs to strengthen overall combat capability

[455] Furthermore, through legal and regulatory studies, working coordination meetings, and joint exercises between ministries, the accumulated mobilization capacity will be combined with emergency response mechanisms such as disaster prevention and relief, infectious disease prevention and control, nuclear accident prevention, and counter-terrorism operations. This will leverage the collective strength of the entire population, strengthen the "national security network" mechanism with the national defense mobilization system as its backup axis, and provide timely support for military operations in wartime to achieve national defense missions

[456] III. Improve the crisis management and response mechanism. The R.O.C. Armed Forces’ “crisis management and response” scope includes “military crises” from the CCP, “terrorist attacks” from within, and “support and coordination for various types of disasters”

[457] Based on this, the concept of "comprehensive security" will continue to be integrated with the operation of the national crisis prevention and response mechanism, guided by the "national security policy," to improve risk management and crisis response capabilities, build a defensive force capable of countering "asymmetric" threats, and, with a pragmatic attitude, emphasize close cooperation between the military and civilians to assist the government in leveraging its capacity for nationwide mobilization to respond to various crises and ensure national security

[458] IV. Building a High-Quality, Efficient, and Modern Military: In order to adapt to future changes in the strategic environment, effectively maintain the military advantage in the Taiwan Strait, and build a high-quality and modern national defense force, the National Armed Forces are actively promoting the "Streamlining Project," a major reform project that effectively reduces the target number of personnel in the National Armed Forces, aiming to make the organizational structure of the forces more streamlined and complete

[459] Throughout its implementation, the plan will incorporate the concept of "military affairs reform" and adhere to the principle of "continuous improvement of combat capabilities." In addition to continuously restructuring national defense organizations and adjusting force structures, strengthening technological leadership, information and electronic advantages, and improving the integration and effectiveness of the three services' weapon systems, it will place particular emphasis on expanding talent channels and improving the quality of human resources, with the aim of building a modern military that is of high quality, efficient, and highly combat-ready

[460] V. Promoting the Policy of Integrating National Defense and People's Livelihood (I) In order to implement the concept of national defense for all citizens and in conjunction with the "Streamlining Project" for combat capability building and deployment, we will continue to promote the policy of "integrating national defense and people's livelihood". This policy will be divided into three aspects: "review and consolidation of military bases", "reduction of the scope of military restricted construction", and "overall planning of training grounds". Under the premise of minimum national defense needs, we will comprehensively review the effectiveness of the overall use of military land, so as to closely integrate people's livelihood and national defense construction in order to cooperate with the overall economic development of the country

[461] Under the guidance of the Executive Yuan, an inter-ministerial coordination mechanism for the development of national defense technology was established to integrate the capabilities of industry, government, academia and research to improve the research and development level and maintenance capabilities of the national defense technology industry

[462] We will continue to expand the scope of defense resource release to businesses, hoping to drive an upward economic cycle, promote economic growth and social prosperity, and achieve a win-win situation for both the military and the people

[463] VI. Strengthening National Defense Technology and Weapon Research and Development (I) In terms of national defense technology research and development, we will continue to utilize existing national defense technology research and development experience and technical capabilities to carry out important scientific research projects to meet the combat needs of the national armed forces and strengthen the overall combat capability of the national armed forces; at the same time, we will establish a logistics system and the ability to manufacture key components, master key technologies, and achieve the goal of "national defense autonomy

[464] ii) In terms of the development of military-civilian integrated technologies, in order to facilitate the exchange between national defense technology and the civilian sector, we will cooperate with the Executive Yuan’s “Industrial Innovation R&D Center Promotion Plan” and “Challenge 2008 National Development Plan” to accelerate military-civilian collaborative R&D projects

[465] III) In terms of future weapon system development, we will prioritize “information and electronic warfare”, “defensive countermeasures weapons”, “joint air defense”, “joint sea control” and “ground defense operations”, focusing on systematic and key-point development, and combining civilian technological capabilities to actively break through key technological bottlenecks

[466] VII. Expanding Regional Military and Security Cooperation. Taiwan is located at the central edge of the western Pacific Ocean and controls the air and sea transportation hub of the Asia-Pacific region. The stability of the Taiwan Strait region is of great strategic interest to the United States and other countries in the Asia-Pacific region

[467] To combine the strengths of regional allies, build a joint defense system, and in line with the "overall diplomacy" policy, we will actively expand military exchanges and cooperation with neighboring countries through official or unofficial channels, thereby increasing our participation in international activities and visibility

[468] To adapt to the shift in modern warfare towards "informatization and technological advancement" and the rapid advancement of weaponry, the development of the armed forces' personnel must move towards specialization and functionalization. Under the guidance of the Executive Yuan's "Comprehensive Review and Improvement Promotion Team for the Military Service System," a concrete plan for military service system reform has been completed to accelerate the reduction of compulsory service and achieve the goal of "primarily recruiting soldiers." To facilitate the implementation of this policy, in addition to continuing the recruitment of volunteer soldiers, adjustments to the officer-soldier ratio of the three branches of the armed forces are being made simultaneously, along with the reduction and merging of recruiting teams for officers and non-commissioned officers, simplification of recruitment procedures, and the planning of a program for selecting outstanding soldiers to become non-commissioned officers

[469] IX. Strengthen the Joint Operations Command Mechanism. In future Taiwan and Penghu defense operations, the PLA may adopt a multi-faceted operational approach that combines advanced technological weapon systems, employing both conventional and unconventional methods, alternating between linear and nonlinear tactics, and relying on both symmetrical and asymmetrical approaches. This approach will be used to launch surprise attacks against Taiwan and Penghu. Therefore, Taiwan and Penghu defense operations will be a joint operations model

[470] To establish a mechanism that ensures consistency between peacetime and wartime operations and aligns authority and responsibility, and to fully leverage joint operations, the National Army continues to integrate "long-range precision strikes" and "synchronous joint engagements" through organizational restructuring and force structure adjustments under the "Refinement Project" and the preparation of the "Victory Project," thereby constructing a "networked" combat capability and effectively unifying the overall combat power of the National Army

[471] Enhance the effectiveness of joint operations training. In order to effectively enhance the joint operations effectiveness of the armed forces and meet the needs of future wars, the armed forces will continue to review and refine the joint operations training courses and implementation methods of the three services, eliminate ineffective training items, and flexibly utilize training methods

[472] In addition, to address the "Enhancement Project's" capability building and "separate regular and rear-area" force deployment, a comprehensive review of the current use of training grounds will be conducted. Forward-looking planning will focus on key areas for troop training and ground facility renovation. Besides meeting the needs of basic troop training grounds and special operations training, a large-scale, comprehensive training ground for all three branches of the armed forces will be constructed using a limited budget, characterized by "generalization, modernization, standardization, and standardization." Furthermore, the procurement of training simulators will continue to meet the needs of grassroots training, and online and multimedia-assisted teaching, including virtual combat scenarios, will be implemented. Furthermore, by combining live-fire exercises with tactical training to verify the results, the frequency and density of training will be increased to accumulate battlefield experience and effectively support joint operations training among the three services

[473] XI. Implement the nationalization and legalization of the military. The National Army is the military of the Republic of China and also the military of all the people. Therefore, it should abide by the constitutional provisions on the "nationalization of the military

[474] Under the central ideology of "fighting for the survival and development of the Republic of China and the safety and well-being of its people," the National Armed Forces have undergone numerous important tests (image omitted: continuous renovation of training grounds, effectively improving training effectiveness) --- Page 92 --- 70 2004 National Defense Report of the Republic of China. Their concrete performance of loyalty to the country, the people, and their duties has won the affirmation and support of all sectors of society

[475] XII. Care for the Families of Outstanding Officers and Soldiers (I) In order to strive for the rationalization of military personnel's treatment and attract outstanding young people to join the military, in addition to actively coordinating to increase the allowance for outlying islands, and in conjunction with the reform of the national tax system, after the amendment of Article 4 of the "Income Tax Act" abolished the tax exemption for military and teacher salaries, the allowance for volunteer service in the national armed forces will be increased to adjust the treatment of military personnel, so as to avoid a disguised pay cut that would affect the morale of military personnel and the lives of military families

[476] II) In order to strengthen medical and health care measures for officers, soldiers and their families, in addition to promulgating the "Regulations on the Implementation of Preferential Treatment (Reduction and Exemption) for Medical Treatment in Military Medical Institutions" and the "Regulations on Subsidies for Medical Expenses of Military Officers, Soldiers and Military Academy Students Injured on Duty" to ensure that officers, soldiers and their families receive timely and appropriate medical services, in addition to cooperating with the government's three-pronged security policy and implementing the "Key Points for Handling Military Dependents' Affairs in the Military", preferential treatment is provided for military dependents and surviving family members to receive medical treatment in military hospitals, so that they can obtain more substantial medical protection

[477] III) In order to consolidate the combat effectiveness of the troops, we will continue to implement the care for officers, soldiers and their families, actively renovate old barracks and living facilities, promote the adjustment of officers' and soldiers' treatment and medical care measures, and accelerate the reconstruction of old military dependents' villages

[478] Section 6 Cross-Strait Military Mutual Trust The national security of our country is closely related to the development of cross-strait relations. Under the current government's overall policy of promoting a "cross-strait peaceful and stable interaction framework," the National Armed Forces, while ensuring national security and avoiding conflict crises, should consult with the other side on a "Code of Conduct in the Taiwan Strait" and pragmatically promote a "cross-strait military mutual trust mechanism" to promote stability and peace in the Taiwan Strait

[479] I. Basic Policy Stance (I) Promoting the “Framework for Peaceful and Stable Cross-Strait Interaction” In his inaugural address on May 20, 2004, President Chen Shui-bian advocated that the future development of cross-strait relations should be based on “one principle (the principle of peace)” and “four major issues (establishing a consultation mechanism, equal and mutually beneficial exchanges, building political relations, and preventing military conflict)”

[480] In his National Day address on October 10th, he further clarified his propositions such as "formally ending the state of hostility between the two sides of the Taiwan Strait," "establishing a cross-strait military mutual trust mechanism," "reviewing cross-strait military policies," and "forming a code of conduct for the Taiwan Strait

[481] Therefore, promoting regional security cooperation, increasing cross-strait military transparency, and fully supporting the government in establishing a cross-strait military mutual trust mechanism through security dialogue and exchanges in order to pursue peace in the Taiwan Strait are important policy goals of the national armed forces

[482] II. Planning Concept for a Military Mutual Trust Mechanism A cross-strait military mutual trust mechanism should be built on the foundation of mutual trust between the two sides. However, the CCP has consistently refused to abandon its intention to use force against Taiwan and has failed to demonstrate concrete goodwill. Therefore, to ensure national security, the mechanism must be planned and implemented in three phases: short-term, medium-term, and long-term (as shown in Figure

[483] a) Short-term phase - "Mutual goodwill and seeking common ground while reserving differences" Continue to express goodwill and strive for international public opinion support

[484] Figure 2-4 Key Points of the Stages for Promoting Cross-Strait Military Mutual Trust Mechanism (Figure omitted: Key points of the stages for promoting cross-strait military mutual trust mechanism: short-term stage - mutual goodwill and seeking common ground while reserving differences; medium-term stage - establishing norms and consolidating mutual trust; long-term stage - ending hostilities and ensuring peace) --- Page 94 --- 72 National Defense Report of the Republic of China

[485] III. Formulation of a "Code of Conduct for the Straits" With "prevention of military conflict" as its main focus, the National Armed Forces generally believe that to reduce misunderstandings and misjudgments between the two sides, avoid accidental military conflicts, promote mutual understanding between the two sides, and ensure the stability of the Straits, it is advisable for both sides to sign a "Code of Conduct for the Straits" to mutually regulate each other. Specific plans are as follows

[486] i) Neither party's aircraft or ships shall engage in radar lock-on, tracking, or other simulated attacks or electronic interference against the other party's aircraft or ships, nor shall they launch any objects at the other party's aircraft or ships

[487] vi) Before conducting exercises and artillery firing on outlying islands such as Kinmen, Matsu, Dongyin, and Wuqiu, as well as the southeastern coast of Fujian, a public announcement shall be made in accordance with international regulations

[488] VII) Emergency Security Procedures Part II National Defense Policy and Military Strategy 1. Jointly develop “emergency security procedures” to reduce uncertainty in crisis response

[489] To ensure long-term peace and stability across the Taiwan Strait, the future will gradually follow the planned process of "establishing a military mutual trust mechanism," promoting military academic exchanges, setting up military buffer zones, and reviewing military policies, weapon quantities and deployments, in order to formally end the state of hostility between the two sides

[490] Section 7 Crisis Management The National Armed Forces are part of the overall national crisis management mechanism. In line with government policy guidance and based on the concept of "comprehensive security," the Armed Forces focus on "military crisis" management. Utilizing the "National Armed Forces Joint Operations Command Mechanism," they closely monitor dynamic and static security factors, striving to achieve the requirements of "crisis prevention, situation monitoring, emergency response, rapid handling, and prevention of escalation," thereby safeguarding the lives and property of the people (as shown in Figure

[493] II) Positioning The Ministry of National Defense's crisis prevention and handling is positioned as the support and coordination agency for major accidents and disasters listed in the "Regulations for Handling Emergencies During the Regular Combat Readiness Period of the National Armed Forces (including unrestricted warfare and counter-terrorism operations)" and the "Disaster Prevention and Relief Act." When an emergency occurs and the competent authority is unable to respond, it will provide timely support as ordered. If the crisis exceeds the defined scope and develops in the direction of military conflict or war, it will act in accordance with relevant operational guidelines and various operational plans. --- Page 96 --- 74 2004 National Defense Report of the Republic of China Figure 2-5 Classification and Responsibility Analysis of Crisis Handling by the Ministry of National Defense President (Commander-in-Chief of the Armed Forces) Exercises Commander-in-Chief Power Directs National Security Council Coordinates Executive Yuan Formulate national defense policies, integrate overall national strength (including human, material, financial and other related resources), and fully support the Ministry of National Defense

[510] According to the "Regulations for Handling Emergencies During the Regular Combat Readiness Period of the National Armed Forces", emergencies such as PLA aircraft, ships or iron-hulled vessels, and motorized fishing vessels exceeding the operational norms of both parties or causing accidents

[511] II. Implementation Strategies and Planning (I) Military Crisis The modern warfare model and the development of high-tech weapons make military crises highly sudden and unpredictable. If the enemy uses "quasi military actions" or adopts terrorist attacks derived from "unrestricted warfare", crisis management will be even more challenging

[512] Accordingly, when the enemy has a clear intent to invade or there are obvious signs of an invasion of Taiwan, the Ministry of National Defense will immediately conduct a comprehensive assessment of the enemy situation and issue an "enhancement of combat readiness" order and "preliminary operational guidance" in a timely manner to respond to the enemy's threat; it will then recommend convening a "National Security Council" in accordance with legal procedures and follow the decision instructions to take appropriate combat readiness measures or carry out defensive combat missions

[513] In order to prepare for the possibility that the enemy may use both conventional and unconventional, linear and nonlinear, and symmetrical and asymmetrical methods to launch a surprise attack on Taiwan, the National Army has completed relevant adjustments during the combat readiness phase. Based on the differences between peacetime and wartime conditions of each unit, it has clearly defined the criteria for action and related combat plans. Adhering to the attitude of "always being cautious about war" and the principle of "never starting a war lightly until the last moment," it has formulated a combat readiness guidance policy and actively completed various combat readiness measures to achieve its defensive combat mission

[514] II) Counter-terrorism operations Terrorist attacks are destructive attacks that are "ideologically driven", "have long-term goals", "are organized and integrated", and "are planned" and employ constantly evolving methods to achieve their radical ambitions and objectives

[515] Accordingly, in order to safeguard the lives and property of the people, the government has completed the overall planning and guidance of the "Taiwan Counter-Terrorism Operation Organization Structure and Operation Mechanism," adopting the "three-three-one" mechanism operation principle: crisis management is divided into three stages: "prevention, handling, and recovery," and risk prevention is divided into three lights: "green, yellow, and red." Once terrorist attack intelligence is confirmed, or a sudden terrorist attack occurs, the counter-terrorism response mechanism will be immediately activated to implement the response

[516] Page 98 --- 76 Republic of China 93 National Defense Report (Image omitted

[517] The National Armed Forces conducted counter-terrorism drills) The National Armed Forces, based on their responsibility to "defend national security," effectively respond to terrorist activities arising from the concept of "unrestricted warfare" and undertake the mission of "counterterrorism and counter-terrorism." If the crisis suddenly escalates into a war conflict, the National Armed Forces will take appropriate combat readiness measures or carry out defensive operations in accordance with relevant operational guidelines and various operational plans

[518] Accordingly, in order to achieve "prevention before it happens" and enhance the effectiveness of "counter-terrorism and crisis response," the National Armed Forces continuously and prudently respond with new concepts and new thinking. In peacetime, they utilize various intelligence gathering systems to collect "intelligence on terrorist activities," and conduct intelligence exchanges both domestically and internationally. Adhering to the principle that "crisis early warning" is more important than "crisis handling and recovery," they actively cooperate with relevant units to seize the initiative and carry out joint counter-terrorism operations to eliminate threats before they materialize

[519] III) Disaster Relief When the country or society encounters a major disaster that exceeds the handling capacity of the disaster management authority or local government, the National Armed Forces shall, in accordance with the "Disaster Prevention and Relief Act" and the "Regulations for Requesting National Armed Forces Support for Disaster Handling", dispatch troops to support disaster relief as soon as possible, under the principles of "not affecting the combat readiness of the National Armed Forces, not disrupting the command system of the National Armed Forces, and not exceeding the support capacity of the National Armed Forces

[520] Since October 2004, the Ministry of National Defense has included "disaster relief" as one of the tasks of the National Army's equipment and supplies, so as to facilitate the legal procurement of necessary disaster relief equipment and machinery and minimize the loss of life and property of the people

[521] III. Military Crisis Prevention Crisis early warning is multifaceted, focusing on "seizing the initiative" and "comprehensive planning," emphasizing "Intelligence monitoring," "crisis prediction," "prepared contingency plans," "early drills," and "capacity reserves

[522] In response to the nature and form of the crisis, the National Armed Forces have closely coordinated with relevant authorities, maintained strict control over various intelligence, prepared contingency plans and response strategies for various emergencies, and guided the three branches of the armed forces to complete the material survey and mobilization preparations in their areas of responsibility. In conjunction with the annual joint military, civilian, and civilian exercises, various comprehensive exercises such as "Joint Territorial Defense" have been carried out to strengthen joint early warning, overall response, and crisis prevention and control capabilities

[523] The National Armed Forces will uphold national security policies and defense objectives and guidelines, monitor the situation in the Taiwan Strait, and strive to reduce military tensions and the risk of miscalculation in the Taiwan Strait, prevent accidental military conflicts, and eliminate crises before they arise

[524] IV. Crisis Management: Focusing on "controlling the development of the crisis" and "quickly quelling it," the emphasis is on "stabilizing the situation," "grasping the core of the crisis," and "integrating response measures

[525] When the country suffers natural or man-made disasters, the National Armed Forces support local governments in implementing disaster relief in accordance with the "Disaster Prevention and Relief Act" and the "Regulations for Applying for National Armed Forces Support in Disaster Management

[526] In the event of a sudden attack by "unrestricted warfare (including terrorist acts)," the nature of the attack should be immediately determined, and the designated response unit should, in accordance with orders, support the competent authorities and local governments to implement "counter-terrorism support" and eliminate the threat as quickly as possible

[527] If the crisis continues to escalate, the Joint Operations Command Center will activate the overall response mechanism and, in accordance with the instructions of the national overall crisis management mechanism, carry out response, countermeasures, or defensive combat missions

[528] In terms of “hardware restoration,” the nation will be mobilized or supported as needed to carry out contamination cleanup or reconstruction tasks to prevent secondary crises from occurring. In terms of “confidence restoration,” efforts will be made to boost morale, strengthen the fighting spirit of the entire nation, and effectively prevent the enemy from launching further attacks

[529] Page 100 --- 78 2004 National Defense Report of the Republic of China --- Page 101 --- Chapter Six Military Strategy Part Two National Defense Policy and Military Strategy 79 Based on the fundamental objectives of "preventing war," "national defense," and "counter-terrorism and counter-terrorism," the National Armed Forces will not only build a military force that is "effective in deterrence and defensive in defense," but will also take control of military leadership, with "knowledge-based military and combat capability advantage" as its development vision, and continue to integrate the three armed forces to make the National Armed Forces a modern force that is of high quality, high efficiency, and strong combat capability, in order to support national defense policy and ensure national security

[530] Section 1 Strategic Concept The National Army's strategic concept includes basic strategic concepts and defense operations guidance. Its purpose is to unite the fighting spirit of the entire nation, integrate the combat capabilities of the three armed forces, give full play to the effectiveness of joint operations, and effectively carry out the defense operations of Taiwan and Penghu

[531] a) Effective deterrence: Establish counteroffensive and defensive capabilities with deterrent effects, and deploy forces that can effectively disrupt or delay the enemy's offensive, so that the enemy, under a rational assessment of war damage, will abandon any attempt to take military action

[532] II) Defensive defense: If the deterrence fails and the enemy launches a military invasion against our country, we will resolutely implement territorial defense with the overall national defense force and the joint combat power of the three armed forces in order to achieve the goals of repelling, repelling and annihilating the enemy

Defense Operations Guidance (as shown in Figure 2-7) --- Page 102 --- 80 National Defense Report of the Republic of China, 2004 Figure 2-7 National Army Defense Operations Guidance Map (Figure omitted: National Army Defense Operations Guidance: Cyber superiority, battlefield awareness, effective countermeasures, stalling enemy offensives, preserving combat power, strategic protracted warfare, joint interception, and territorial defense) (I) Cyber superiority and battlefield awareness In order to obtain early warning and grasp the enemy's movements, the cyber offensive and defensive strategies, preemptive soft kills, and chain systems are combined Establish a tactical data integration platform to facilitate the integration of joint intelligence, surveillance, intelligence behind enemy lines, and intelligence from allies, enabling close monitoring to create opportunities, establish momentum, and achieve joint operational objectives

[535] II) Effectively counter and hinder enemy offensives. Utilize cyber warfare capabilities, long-range precision weapons, and a portion of naval and air force forces, combined with special agents behind enemy lines and combat capabilities on outlying islands, to quickly paralyze key enemy nodes, disrupt the invading enemy's operational rhythm, and thwart the invading enemy's attempt to achieve a decisive victory in the first battle and a swift victory

[536] III) Preservation of combat power and strategic sustainability The National Army must strengthen its combat power preservation measures, such as multiple backup, dispersed deployment, comprehensive protection and rapid response mobility, to counter the enemy's asymmetrical combat actions of sudden attacks, reduce the damage of enemy attacks, maintain its combat capability, and support the strategic sustainability of the war

[537] iv) Joint Interception and Territorial Defense: The three services will make integrated use of long-range, medium-range, and short range troops and firepower, leveraging superior communication and information capabilities, to conduct joint interception operations when the enemy launches a surprise attack. This will concentrate combat power in a favorable combat area, gradually weaken the enemy's combat power, cut off and destroy the invading enemy, and ensure territorial security. For ground defense, special operations forces will be combined with reserve forces to use rapid reaction mobility to contain enemy sabotage and maintain the integrity of combat power and the security of important political and economic construction

[538] Section 2 Military Development Planning In response to changes in the strategic environment and the needs of military defense operations, the National Armed Forces, following the concept of "military affairs innovation," promotes future military development planning and builds a high-quality, modern armed force through the methods of "gradual progress" and "pragmatic reform

The system will be upgraded to improve the weapon systems of the three armed forces and integrate joint combat capabilities, thereby enhancing the overall combat power of the national armed forces

Combat Training and Preparation – Force Combat Planning: Anticipate enemy threats, envision future combat scenarios, carefully plan annual exercises and training, thoroughly review obstacles to military buildup and combat readiness, and thereby study relevant combat plans, test joint command mechanisms, strengthen wargaming system functions, and improve joint command and control effectiveness

[552] Political warfare aims to enhance intangible combat capabilities and ensure national security; to maintain military morale and ensure the will to resist the enemy, thereby consolidating our own position; to use psychological and strategic warfare to counter the PLA's non-military "three wars" against us—"legal warfare," "public opinion warfare," and "psychological warfare"; and to use mass warfare to build a national defense force to protect our hometown, home, and property, thereby supporting the achievement of the mission of defending the territory

[553] The relevant planning objectives are as follows: --- Page 106 --- 84 National Defense Report of the Republic of China, 2004 (I) Maintaining the morale of the military

[554] Section 3. National Armed Forces Strategy In order to implement the "lean force policy" and achieve a comprehensive transformation of its force structure, the National Armed Forces, in addition to reviewing the implementation and operation of the National Defense Law and its organizational structure, carried out organizational restructuring at the senior command level to achieve the goals of "organizational improvement" and "comprehensive transformation." Furthermore, it proactively anticipates enemy threats and operational needs, and implements force restructuring to ensure that the National Armed Forces maintain a "qualitative" advantage in both personnel quality and weaponry

[555] I. Force Structure Adjustment (I) Army In accordance with the "separation of regular and rear" principle, the existing core and mobilization units will be reviewed and transferred to the reserve command. In accordance with the acquisition schedule of weapons and operational needs, and in conjunction with the merging of outlying island units, the number of regular units will be adjusted and fully staffed to establish a ground combat capability of "rapid response, mobile strike, and effective annihilation

[556] ii) Navy: Based on operational missions, geographical and hydrological environment, force characteristics, and the acquisition schedule of large warships and submarines, the Navy plans to consolidate existing fleets and form a submarine fleet, while improving the organizational structure of the Marine Corps Command to establish a "highly efficient, high-quality, rapid-deployment, and long-range strike" sea control capability

[557] III) Air Force: Based on the results of the "combat team" equipment experiment, review the organization of each wing, plan the merging of bases and wings of the same type of aircraft; and based on the acquisition schedule of electronic reconnaissance, defensive radar early warning equipment, etc., continue to adjust the organizational structure of the battle control, meteorology, and communications wings in order to establish an air superiority capability of "early warning, defense against enemy surprise attacks, and long-range engagement

[558] iv) Joint Logistics In accordance with the guidance of the logistics system adjustment, plan to transfer subordinate production plants, surveying and measurement units to the Armaments Bureau; incorporate regional supply, fuel, ammunition, transportation and medical units of the three services, and integrate them with their subordinate bases to form "regional support command headquarters"; the ammunition section, supply, transportation and medical section under the Army Logistics Command are transferred to the Joint Logistics Department to establish an ammunition section and a medical section, giving full play to the professional preparation functions of ammunition and medical services, and --- Page 107 --- Part Two National Defense Policy and Military Strategy 85 Enhance the support capacity of "resource sharing" through "information, logistics automation, integrated repair, proactive delivery and supply

[559] iv) Reserves In accordance with the principle of “separation of regular and reserve forces”, the organization and number of regional (county/city) reserve commands and reserve brigades (including mountain reserve companies) will be adjusted to build a national defense reserve force; and the organizational structure of reserve brigades will be reviewed and adjusted in accordance with the annual new recruit training needs to take over the new recruit training tasks of the ground forces of the three services

[560] vi) Military Police: In coordination with the national crisis management mechanism, strengthen counter-terrorism preparedness; be responsible for “garrison, special services, maintenance of military discipline and support of the three services’ operations”, review the reduction of regional investigation teams, and implement organizational restructuring and staff reduction

[561] II. Organizational Adjustments at the Ministry of National Defense Senior Divisions: In accordance with the legislative spirit of the two National Defense Laws, the organization of the military administration system is primarily aimed at fulfilling the mission of building up the armed forces; the military command system is primarily aimed at carrying out military operations and preparing for war, while strengthening intelligence, operations, and communications functions and organization; the arms system is responsible for arms preparation; and the headquarters of each service (general) are planned and adjusted with the goal of supporting military operations and implementing the military building philosophy

[562] III. Strengthening the Joint Operations Command Mechanism: Based on the verification and review of the "Han Kuang" and "Lian Hsing" exercises, the ROC Armed Forces have completed the adjustment and revision of the organizational structure of the "Joint Operations Command Mechanism," which is divided into two major systems: "Joint Operations Command" and "Operational Support." Through "multicommunication networks," the joint operations effectiveness is achieved by "simplifying troop command, flattening command hierarchy, increasing command speed," and "rapidly enhancing war sustainability support

[563] a) The “Joint Operations Command System” is divided into three levels: “Strategic Decision-Making and Command (Joint Operations Command Center), Strategic Execution (Ten Strategic Execution Units), and Operational Forces”

[564] ii) The “Operational Support System” includes the “Political Coordination Center and War Resources Coordination Center” of the Ministry of National Defense and the “Comprehensive Coordination Center for All Services”

[565] III) The “Joint Operations Command System” is centered on the “Joint Operations Command Center”, under which the Chief of the General Staff carries out military command on the orders of the Minister. It has a 'needs-and-supports' coordination relationship with the “Political Affairs Coordination Center” and the “War Resources Coordination Center”, which are respectively directed by the “Military Affairs” and “Arms” Deputy Ministers, so as to fully grasp and manage the war surface and effectively integrate and exert the overall national combat power

[566] Enhanced joint operations command mechanism: COC, RTOC, NOC/FOC/SOC, AOC/ROCC; strategic decision-making, joint operations command system, operations command C2, service command system, ISR, intelligence integration decision support, combat support C&C, database, tactical execution

[567] Reorganization of the Main Weapons and Equipment of the National Armed Forces (I) The Main Equipment Acquired by the National Armed Forces The reorganization of the National Armed Forces’ specific combat capabilities is based on the overall planning of the “Ten-Year Armed Forces Development Plan” and the “Forces Reorganization Plan”, and the reorganization goals are achieved in stages according to the priority of military buildup and preparation

[568] In recent years, the Republic of China Armed Forces have successively completed the commissioning and combat readiness of air superiority capabilities (Ching-kuo, F-16 and Mirage fighter jets) and part of the sea control capabilities (Lafayette and Cheng Kung-class frigates

[569] Currently, the planned construction schedule includes the development of an automated command and control system (the Po-Sheng Project), Kidd-class destroyers, long-range reconnaissance radar, Thunder 2000 multiple launch rockets, and amphibious landing vehicles. In the future, with the development of ballistic missile defense systems (Patriot III missile systems), subsequent naval control capabilities (P-3C long-range fixed-wing anti-submarine aircraft and diesel-electric submarines), new attack helicopters, and self-propelled artillery, the Taiwan Strait's military balance will be effectively maintained, the cost of Chinese military action against Taiwan will be increased, and the goals of preventing and deterring war will be achieved, thus ensuring national security

[570] II) Key Points of the ROC Armed Forces' Weapon and Equipment Restructuring In the face of the PRC's escalating military threat to Taiwan, the ROC Armed Forces completed the restructuring of its air superiority and some naval control capabilities between 1993 and 2002. At present, the focus is on the restructuring of C4ISR, missile defense, and naval control capabilities

[571] Part Two: National Defense Policy and Military Strategy 87 Based on comprehensive defense strategy considerations, after meticulous planning and phased implementation, and considering that the annual national defense budget cannot meet the country's defense needs in a timely manner, the Ministry of National Defense planned to procure three urgently needed pieces of equipment for combat readiness in 2004 by allocating a special budget: "Patriot III Missile System", "Diesel-Electric Submarine", and "Long-Range Fixed-Wing Anti-Submarine Aircraft

[572] If acquired according to the planned schedule, it will effectively enhance Taiwan's active anti-missile defense capabilities, counter enemy maritime blockades and amphibious operations, increase the flexibility of defensive operations, and build basic strategic deterrence capabilities, thereby achieving the strategic goal of "effective deterrence and defensive fortification" and maintaining the military balance in the Taiwan Strait

[573] Section 4. Vision of the National Armed Forces The vision of the National Armed Forces is a plan that combines the Executive Yuan’s national development strategy vision and uses performance evaluation to gradually and effectively build a modern national defense force

[574] I. National Armed Forces Vision – “Knowledge-Based Military, Combat Advantage” (I) Knowledge-Based Military The specific meaning of “Knowledge-Based Military” is that the National Armed Forces take knowledge as the guide and adopt the concept of innovation to carry out military affairs reform in military thinking, military theory, military system, military technology, military management, organizational system and combat training, comprehensively promote military transformation, and build a modern combat capability based on knowledge

[575] II) Combat Advantage In response to the evolution of future warfare, the ROC Armed Forces will enhance its integrated soft and hard combat capabilities based on "knowledge-based military" principles. This includes strengthening reconnaissance, command and communication, mobility, strike capability, and logistical support. The goal is to establish a joint operations force that is highly mobile (speed), highly adaptable (flexible), and possesses strong strike capability (combat effectiveness). This will enable the ROC Armed Forces to quickly seize battlefield dominance during wartime, thereby gaining a combat advantage and defeating any invading enemy

[576] Illustration omitted: Knowledge military creates superior combat power) II. Vision and Program (I) Professionalism The legislative spirit of the two national defense laws emphasizes professional division of labor, especially --- Page 110 --- 88 Republic of China 93 National Defense Report It is with the development of high technology that the division of labor is becoming more detailed and the functions are becoming more professional

[577] II) Transformation In the face of the future multidimensional, digital, noncontact, universal, and unrestricted warfare, the National Army will transform into a "high-quality, efficient, and powerful" national defense force, guided by knowledge

[578] The relevant strategies are as follows: 1. Reform the organization and structure

[579] III) Speed The organization, structure and equipment of the National Army must emphasize the creation of "speed" to enable flexible engagement or action capabilities, take the initiative in battlefield situation and target selection, and have a stronger response capability to improve battlefield survival rate and target achievement rate

[580] The relevant strategies are as follows: 1. Adjust the organizational structure

The national defense system of the Republic of China is structured as follows: President, National Security Council, Executive Yuan, and Ministry of National Defense

[585] II. After the implementation of the two national defense laws, the national defense organization will be structured as follows: the military-political system will be responsible for military building and strategic planning; the arms system will be responsible for arms preparation; and the military command system will be responsible for troop command and combat operations

[586] III. The standing forces of the National Armed Forces: The Army is responsible for the defense of the main island and outlying islands during peacetime, implementing ground combat readiness (including countermeasures), and conducting joint ground defense operations with friendly forces during wartime; the Navy conducts maritime patrols during peacetime to ensure maritime security and strengthen combat capabilities

[587] In wartime, the joint forces will conduct joint operations with allied forces; the Air Force will be responsible for patrolling the Taiwan Strait in peacetime, maintaining the security of the Taiwan Strait airspace, strengthening combat capabilities, and striving for air superiority and operational effectiveness in wartime; joint logistics will establish a wartime logistics mechanism to support the establishment and deployment of the armed forces; the military police will perform special guard, counter-terrorism operations, garrison duties, and support the operations of the three services to ensure national security; missile units will conduct combat readiness preparations in peacetime, and in wartime, integrate land-based medium- and long-range air defense, sea control missiles, and precision long-range attack forces to carry out source countermeasures, missile defense, and joint interception operations; the cyber warfare units will perform... It controls the cybersecurity and monitoring of the command system, prevents enemy network intrusion, sabotage, blocking, and theft during wartime, effectively utilizes electronic warfare support and electronic warfare protection, and carries out strategic and tactic countermeasures as ordered to gain cybersecurity advantage

[588] IV. The Reserve Command is responsible for the organization, management, equipment, mobilization, and training of the National Army's ground reserve forces, strengthening mobilization and preparation to enable them to have the combat capability for regional defense and protecting their hometowns, land, and property, thus fulfilling the mission of national defense

[589] The implementation of the two National Defense Laws established the connotations of "unified military administration and military command", "civilian leadership of the military", and "professional division of labor among military administration, military equipment, and military command", and constructed a national defense organization with "matching rights and responsibilities" and "layered professionalism", so that the armed forces can focus on combat training and combat capability preparation, and become a modern and high-quality military

[590] II. Powers and Responsibilities (I) President The President commands the Army, Navy and Air Force of the whole country and is the Commander-in-Chief of the three armed forces. He exercises the power of command and directs the military. He is directly responsible to the Minister of National Defense, who orders the Chief of the General Staff to command and execute the command (Article 8 of the National Defense Act

[591] ii) National Security Council The President may convene a National Security Council to decide on national security-related national defense policies or to respond to major national defense emergencies (Article 9 of the National Defense Act

[592] III) Executive Yuan The Executive Yuan formulates national defense policies, integrates the overall national strength, and supervises its subordinate agencies in handling national defense-related matters (Article 10 of the National Defense Act

[593] Ministry of National Defense 1. The Ministry of National Defense is in charge of national defense affairs; it should give full play to its professional functions in military administration, military equipment and military command, and make recommendations on national defense policies and formulate military strategies based on the needs of national defense (Article 11 of the National Defense Act

[600] Section 2 Organization of the Ministry of National Defense I. Organizational System of the Ministry of National Defense The organization of the Ministry of National Defense has undergone tremendous changes since the implementation of the "National Defense Act" and the amended "Organization Act of the Ministry of National Defense

[601] Based on the division of labor, it is divided into three major systems: military administration, military equipment, and military command. The military administration system is responsible for making recommendations on national defense policies and military strategic planning. The military equipment system is responsible for managing military equipment preparation. The military command system is responsible for military orders and commanding the troops to perform its professional functions

[602] II. Military and Political System. The military and political system consists of 14 internal units, including the Strategic Planning Division, and 29 directly subordinate agencies (organizations), as well as a military academy. It is overseen by the Vice Minister in charge of military and political affairs, acting on the orders of the Minister. Their responsibilities and the system are described below

[658] Part Three National Defense Organization and Armed Forces Chapter Nine Reserve Forces In order to cope with the high-tech warfare of the future and the development of the CCP's "nonlinear, non-contact, and asymmetric" combat capabilities, the Armed Forces take "technology leadership, cyber superiority, joint interception, and territorial defense" as the guiding principles for building the army. Based on the concept of "standing combat and reserve defense", and in conjunction with the implementation of the "Streamlining Project", the military organizational structure is adjusted with the "separation of standing and reserve" as the orientation. The troops are clearly divided into two systems: standing and reserve, which comprehensively enhances the emergency combat capabilities of the standing forces and achieves the goal of rapid response and change control

[659] Section 1 Coastal Defense Reserve Forces I. Duties In peacetime, they are responsible for the first phase of recruit training and mobilization; in wartime, they are responsible for frontline coastal defense

[660] Coastal Defense, Urban and Rural Defense. II. Organization: In response to the separation of regular and reserve forces, the Army's 8 coastal defense (new recruit) brigades, 9 independent mobilization battalions, and the Marine Corps New Recruitment Center were transferred to the Reserve Command. In addition, 4 reserve forward brigades were coordinated to form a joint infantry and artillery coastal defense force according to the defense and combat needs of each theater of operations. The brigade (battalion) is the unit of origin. In peacetime, a standing backbone is organized and subordinate to the reserve command of each region. In wartime, it is mobilized and organized and incorporated into the operational system of the theater of operations for control and use

[662] Part Three National Defense Organization and National Armed Forces Section Two Urban and Rural Defense Reserve Forces I. Missions In peacetime, they carry out mobilization and preparation; in wartime, they undertake urban and rural defense or deep-penetration defense tasks in various combat zones

[663] Section 3 Reserve Forces for Key Area Protection I. Duties During peacetime, they are responsible for mobilization and preparation; during wartime, they are responsible for the defense of low and high mountain platforms in various operational zones

[664] Key Area Defense II. Organization: Composed of 29 mountain companies and 5 high-altitude platform companies, etc., based on the needs of defending shallow mountain passes and key military points in each combat zone, adopting a light infantry form, with independent companies as the parent unit, forming a standing core in peacetime, subordinate to the reserve command of each region; in wartime, mobilization and organization are carried out and incorporated into the combat system of the combat zone for control and operation

[665] Section 4 Expanding and Mobilizing Reserve Forces I. Missions During peacetime, they will carry out service education and training or mobilization and preparation according to the missions and nature of their respective parent units; in wartime, they will undertake missions such as defense of sea and air bases, factory and warehouse security, reserve forces in combat zones, or combat support and logistical support

[666] II. Organization Based on the various military schools, testing centers, military police, air force guards, joint logistics port services, and motor transport units, each unit, according to the characteristics of its military branch, uses the current personnel allocation method to organize the core of reserve forces such as brigades, combat support, and logistical support at the brigade, group, command, or independent battalion levels. These reserve forces are subordinate to the regional reserve command. During peacetime, they are assigned to their original military branches; during wartime, they are mobilized and integrated into the operational system of the theater of operations for control and utilization

[667] Section 5 Auxiliary Military Service Units I. Missions: To assist in military services or local self-defense and air defense during wartime or in case of emergency

[668] Section 6 Future Outlook I. Under the policy of "continuous strike capability and reserve defense capability", the National Army will supervise and implement the "organization, management, equipment, mobilization and training" of the National Army's ground reserve forces through the Reserve Command, so as to implement mobilization and preparation, and enable them to have the combat capability for regional defense and protection of hometowns, territories and assets, and to achieve the mission of national defense

[669] Page 146 --- 124 2004 National Defense Report of the Republic of China --- Page 147 --- Part Four: National Defense Resources and Management --- Page 148 --- 126 2004 National Defense Report of the Republic of China Summary of this Part I. The goals and manpower structure of the National Armed Forces will be planned in the direction of "the standing army mainly composed of recruits and the reserve army mainly composed of conscriptions," and the ratio of recruits to conscriptions will be adjusted appropriately to meet the needs of modern warfare

[670] Part Four National Defense Resources and Management Chapter Ten National Defense Manpower In response to the development of the national political and economic situation and the implementation of the "Streamlining Project", the National Armed Forces are planning national defense manpower in the direction of "a system of recruitment and conscription with recruitment as the main force". In conjunction with the restructuring of national defense organizations, the upgrading of weapons and equipment and human resource management, the quality of personnel will be improved in order to build a modern army with high quality, appropriate quantity and strong combat power

[671] Section 1 National Defense Human Resources Planning In order to implement national defense policies, coordinate with the reorganization of the armed forces, and give full play to the overall national strength, our national defense human resources planning is as follows: 1. The armed forces' strength objectives and human resources structure will be planned in accordance with the direction of "the standing army is mainly composed of recruits and the reserve army is mainly composed of conscriptions," and the ratio of recruitment to conscription will be appropriately adjusted to meet the needs of modern warfare

[672] Section 2 Sources and Structure of National Defense Personnel National defense personnel are divided into two parts: civilian and military personnel. Civilian personnel are recruited through the "National Examination" and "Open Selection"; military personnel are recruited through conscription and recruitment

[673] Section 3 National Defense Human Resource Management National defense human resource management needs to take into account the overall development of the country and make the most economical and effective allocation and use

[693] IV. Vocational Training for Veterans To support veterans retiring from the armed forces and ensure their smooth integration into national development, maximizing the overall effectiveness of the nation's human resources, the Ministry of National Defense organizes vocational training services annually through four channels: (1) In collaboration with the Youth Counselling Council, the Council of Labor Affairs, and the Veterans Affairs Council, the Ministry of National Defense organizes "Mobile Information Sessions on Employment Services for Retired Veterans," offering recruitment and job-seeking services and consultations based on the location of the troops stationed in the North, Central, South, East, Penghu, Kinmen, and Matsu regions

[694] IV. In order to promote the conscription system, we will cooperate with the Taoyuan Vocational Training Center of the Veterans Affairs Council to offer training courses in mechanics, water and electricity, auto repair, computer and fire management, etc., to provide training for retired volunteer soldiers so that they can learn various skills to serve the society, or, according to their personal wishes, be guided to employment and receive scholarships from the Veterans Affairs Council

[695] Section Four: Policy Direction I. Improve the Civilian System According to the "Organization Act of the Ministry of National Defense," the number of civilian personnel appointed to the Ministry of National Defense headquarters shall not be less than one-third of the authorized personnel quota. This aims to ensure that outstanding civilian personnel are involved in national defense affairs and the formulation of national defense policies. Therefore, their appointment is planned in accordance with current laws and regulations, taking into account the use of professional and skilled personnel, and supplemented by comprehensive and appropriate measures such as "promotion management," "rewards and punishments assessment," and "education and training," so that they are placed in suitable positions to give full play to their strengths. Furthermore, under a good military-civilian relationship, the national defense civilian system will develop steadily

[696] National Defense Report of the Republic of China, 2004 IV. Comprehensive Improvement of Human Resource Quality To improve human resource quality, the National Army, guided by the principle of "quality over quantity," recruits high-quality personnel through professional marketing and diversified methods to serve in the military

[697] National Defense Resources and Management Chapter 11 National Defense Financial Resources National defense financial resources are determined by balancing multiple factors such as overall national economic development, government financial burden, and national security. It also considers the current enemy threat and current national defense policy, and is based on the financial management guidelines formed by the national armed forces' planning and budgeting system. This is to implement national defense financial planning, adhere to the principle of "lean and economical", consider the priority of military buildup and preparation, effectively plan the national defense budget and properly allocate national defense financial resources, effectively support combat readiness and preparation tasks in stages and year by year, implement national defense expenditure management, and achieve the set national defense goals

[698] Section 1 National Defense Financial Planning In accordance with the Executive Yuan's medium- and long-term planning budget system and the medium-term budget amount approved by the Executive Yuan, and adhering to the principle of "plans equal budgets", the national defense medium-term financial planning was completed; and in accordance with the "National Armed Forces Planning Budget System" medium- and long-term overall planning, the priority of military building and preparation was taken into account, and financial resources were properly allocated to support military missions in a timely manner

[699] Section 2 National Defense Budget The national defense budget is a necessary investment to safeguard national security. Only by comprehensively reviewing policy priorities, reducing personnel and general administrative expenditures, and prioritizing the needs of combat readiness missions and the procurement of high-tech weapons and equipment can the budget's effectiveness be fully realized

[711] III. Comparison of Increases and Decreases in National Defense Budget Structure, Personnel Maintenance, Operational Maintenance, and Military Investment over the Past Decade The annual budget requirements of the Ministry of National Defense have been comprehensively reviewed and prioritized according to policy priorities, with efficient allocation of various needs. Personnel maintenance, in accordance with the Executive Yuan's policy directives, prioritizes fulfilling all legally mandated obligations, including salaries, insurance, food allowances, retirement benefits, and various subsidies for military dependents for military and civilian personnel. Operational maintenance focuses on maintaining basic combat capabilities, prioritizing the fulfillment of various weapons, equipment, and spare parts with "combat readiness missions." The military investment plan covers the needs for replacement, repair, and combat readiness training fuel and ammunition, while also taking into account the maintenance of facilities for the "basic living" of officers and soldiers, medical care, clothing, and water and electricity expenditures, as well as the needs for "general operations" service support, political warfare, administrative operations, and the first reserve fund. In addition, military investment is based on the military building guidelines and force reorganization plan, focusing on "information and electronic warfare", "missile defense system" and "naval combat capability", and rigorously reviewing the minimum requirements of the continuation plan and prioritizing the inclusion of major arms sales (as shown in Figures 4-9 and
