[1068] II. Parliamentary Coordination and Implementation Effectiveness: To establish a complete parliamentary liaison system, the Ministry of National Defense has set up a Parliamentary Affairs Office at its headquarters; each service has set up a Parliamentary Group to be responsible for parliamentary coordination and liaison work

[1069] I) Legislative Yuan: The Ministry of National Defense interacts with the highest public opinion body through policy implementation, special reports, responses to inquiries, inspections, visits, and public hearings (symposiums, coordination meetings, explanations), so that legislators can understand the actual situation of the national armed forces’ construction and preparation for war. At the same time, under its guidance and assistance, the Ministry effectively promotes national defense work

[1070] Part 8 Important National Defense Policies (V) Civil-Military Relations items

[1071] II) Control Yuan: From July 2002 to November 2004, the Control Yuan accepted 172 cases for investigation and arranged for Control Yuan members to inspect military units 29 times

[1072] Section 2 News Liaison Under a democratic political system, it is normal for national defense affairs to be subject to public scrutiny. In line with the trend of the times, the Ministry of National Defense adheres to the principle of "transparency in national defense administration" without violating the principle of military secrets, and takes into account the public's right to "know". Through the military's news liaison channels, it explains and communicates to the public, so that the public can have a deep understanding of various national defense policies and actions, build a consensus on "national defense for all", and strive for the public's support and affirmation of the military

[1073] I. National Armed Forces News Policy The National Armed Forces' current news policy is based on four principles: "safeguarding national security and defense interests as a prerequisite," "building national defense consensus and establishing good military-civilian relations as a requirement," "cooperating with government policies and national defense administration as a principle," and "achieving the mission of the National Armed Forces and current military tasks as an objective." Through effective interaction and communication with the media, a bridge is built to reach the public, promote national defense policies, establish a correct understanding of national defense among the people, and ultimately support the National Armed Forces' military building and combat readiness efforts

[1074] II. News Handling Mechanism The military spokesperson system serves as a window for external communication and an important mechanism for interaction with the media

[1075] Spokespersons are appointed by each unit of the National Armed Forces as follows: (I) The Ministry of National Defense appoints a military spokesperson to speak on behalf of the Ministry of National Defense to the outside world

[1076] III. Key Points of News Operations National defense policies are complex and multifaceted, and are related to the rights, obligations, security, and well-being of the people. Therefore, the Ministry of National Defense adopts a proactive, open, and responsible attitude in explaining all policies to the public. Regarding national defense and military issues of public concern, the Ministry adheres to the principles of "prudence, detail, comprehensiveness, accuracy, and timeliness," and adopts a proactive, immediate, and honest approach to communicate, clarify, or refute information to the outside world as soon as possible to prevent negative effects from escalating and spreading, effectively maintaining the positive image of the national armed forces. The key points of its operations are as follows: (I) Close Contact and Immediate Communication Establish comprehensive information and close communication channels with the media. In addition to handling reporters' applications for interviews, appointments, news inquiries, and providing background information, we also hold regular and irregular press conferences, release press releases, and arrange invitations, visits, seminars, social gatherings, and media visits. We strive to proactively disclose national defense and military information without violating confidentiality and security, strengthen communication, and promote media relations to facilitate the advancement of our work. This will enable the public to be widely aware of the overall military buildup and preparedness efforts and various policy achievements of the armed forces through the power of media dissemination, thereby contributing to the cohesion and strengthening of national defense consensus among the entire population

[1077] II) Extensive Collection and Appropriate Handling of Public Opinion Based on respect for public opinion and a deep understanding of the public's expectations for national defense policies, all levels of the armed forces collect, grasp and understand the news and public opinion responses reported by various media such as print, electronic and online media in a timely manner

[1078] III) Clarify false news immediately. The most important thing in handling news is "timeliness". If false reports are allowed to continue to be published and ferment, it will seriously damage the image of the national army and cause harm to the country and society

[1079] iv) Strengthen the role of news coverage of military exercises. Any military exercise or training activity is always of great concern to the public. Therefore, we have always taken the initiative to conduct thorough publicity and planning of news operations in advance. Through various means such as inviting people, holding press conferences or issuing press releases, we proactively explain the purpose, characteristics and relevant background of the exercise or training, and provide the media with accurate and sufficient information in order to expand the effect of news publicity and achieve the goal of affirming and supporting national defense

[1080] IV. Implementation Results (as shown in Table 8-6) (I) Press Releases and Q&A Press Releases: Press release operations are conducted in accordance with the principles of initiative, speed, accuracy, and confidentiality. All major national defense policies and activities, including personnel, intelligence, operational plans, logistics, communications, political warfare, manpower, resources, legal system, armaments, accounting, military law, and military medicine, as well as enemy movements, fall under the military category. Table 8-6: Statistics on the Implementation of Military Press Work Item News Work Item Results I. Military Press Releases 372 items II. Press Q&A 3,528 items III. Regular Press Conferences 101 sessions IV. Visits and Receptions to Domestic and Foreign Media 62 times Data date: 2004 (Republic of China year

[1081] II) Regular Press Conferences The Ministry of National Defense holds regular press conferences every Tuesday morning, chaired by a military spokesperson. The conferences proactively address important national defense policy issues or news topics of media concern, and invite representatives from the Ministry of National Defense and various branches of the armed forces

[1082] III) Media Invitations: The Armed Forces distinguish between planned and ad-hoc invitations, and invite media regularly or irregularly each year by the Ministry of National Defense or the various branches of the armed forces

[1083] The headquarters planned to invite media to visit various levels of military units for on-site reporting of important military exercises, reserve mobilization, weapons development, training results demonstrations, important celebrations, outstanding units, and exemplary officers and soldiers. From July 2002 to November 2004, domestic and foreign media were invited to visit military units a total of 62 times. These included visits to annual model units of the armed forces, tours of military academies, briefings on defense resources, presidential inspections of various military units, the armed forces' support for SARS prevention and emergency response and the completion and opening of Songshan Hospital, the commissioning ceremony of the Army Aviation and Special Forces Command's CH-47SD helicopter air transport battalion, and the 2003 Yunlin Sixth Naphtha Cracking Plant's "Anti-Terrorism Exercise" (Part 8). Important National Defense Policies (V) Military-Civilian Relations 295, including the "Han Kuang 19 Exercise" joint attack drill, the unity training ceremony for recruits of the three services and six schools and female military training instructors, the military education and training review meeting, the implementation and lottery of the National Defense Internet quiz, the verification of the national armed forces' weapons systems, the groundbreaking ceremony for the "Bo Ai Project", and the media visit to the national armed forces during the Lunar New Year to enhance their vigilance. Through media reports, the national armed forces are shown to be diligently trained, well-trained and strong, so that the people can understand the effectiveness of the national armed forces' combat readiness

[1084] IV. Media Liaison The National Army strengthens its liaison and coordination with the media on a regular basis, using methods such as "telephone, personal visits, meetings, and providing information" to achieve the goal of "being friends with the media" through positive daily interactions and friendly and efficient service

[1085] V. Policy Direction (I) Implementing the Image Marketing Concept With the concept of "image marketing" and innovative, dynamic public relations skills, we will break free from the constraints of conservatism and passivity, improve our news reporting, showcase the effectiveness of national defense and military building policies, build national defense consensus, and enable the people to support and participate in national defense based on their understanding of national defense policies, thus becoming a strong backing for national security

[1086] II) Leveraging the Integrated News Mechanism: News processing must anticipate crises, grasp the situation, and respond to media demands in the shortest time and with the highest efficiency. Therefore, all units of the Joint Staff Department of the Ministry of National Defense and the General Headquarters of each service strive to build an integrated news processing mechanism through flexible command and smooth coordination, so as to fully support and cooperate with news releases and achieve the expected goals of "crisis management." At the same time, with an eye toward the goal of "peacetime-wartime integration," we strive to ensure that news operations meet the needs of the National Armed Forces' operations and the future development trends of news warfare

[1087] III) Improve News Processing Efficiency Implement the requirement that all units of the National Armed Forces establish a "24-hour standby" news processing concept, regardless of weekdays, holidays, or working hours, to respond and handle news appropriately at any time; any negative or false reports that affect the image and morale of the National Armed Forces should be clarified and refuted promptly to gain external support. (IV) Strengthen Media Service and Liaison The news media is a bridge for communication between the National Armed Forces and the public, and also an important force in urging the National Armed Forces to make progress

[1088] Section 3 Military-Civilian Friendship To promote unity and harmony among the troops and to strengthen the inherent bond of "military and civilians as one family," the armed forces at all levels have conducted activities such as family visits, social gatherings, and family services. In a warm and harmonious communication environment, officers, soldiers' families, and people from all walks of life have gained a deeper understanding of the significant and concrete achievements of the armed forces, such as "respect for the law and pragmatism," "diligent training and excellence," and "sound and reasonable discipline

[1089] I. Implementation Results (I) Troop Display In order to showcase the achievements of military building and combat readiness and win the hearts of the people, the way the armed forces display their troops is to be appropriately planned and carried out by each new recruit training and education unit and the troops in the main island and outlying islands, according to the characteristics and missions of the unit (defense zone) and in combination with holidays, team anniversaries, birthday parties, weapons verification of "Han Kuang Exercise" and open days of the barracks

[1090] II) Family Reunions and Friendships: To ensure that conscripted families understand the current service status of their reservists, promote two-way communication between the military and their families, establish positive interaction, and resolve concerns, all levels of the armed forces organize family reunions and friendship gatherings at their respective barracks, taking advantage of occasions such as holidays, unit anniversaries, birthday celebrations, and open house events. These gatherings invite officers' and soldiers' families, relatives, friends, local leaders, and representatives from conscription agencies to participate. Through warm, friendly, and emotional exchanges and interactions, officers' and soldiers' families gain insight into their children's lives in the barracks, reassuring parents and comrades alike, and strengthening combat effectiveness

[1091] In 2003-2004, a total of 4,882 events were held, with more than 450,000 family members participating

[1092] III) Services for the Families of Regular Soldiers To assist in resolving difficulties related to conscription and family members, the Republic of China Armed Forces, through open barracks, showcases the combat capabilities of its troops and enhances services for the families of officers and soldiers. Through the Republic of China Military Friends Association and military service stations in various counties and cities, it assists in handling various conscription and family service work such as "liaison and patrol visits", "conscription and family member contact services", "case coordination and discipline", and "dispute and litigation assistance". It also assists in establishing "Military Friends Service Centers" to provide officers, soldiers and their families with consultation channels on rights and obligations, so as to stabilize the morale of soldiers, unite the people, and boost the morale of the military

[1093] IV) Selection of Role Models In order to establish role models of the armed forces who love the people, inspire officers and soldiers to love the people, promote ethics and morality, and rectify social atmosphere, this selection aims to commend representatives of good people and good deeds and role models who love the people, and to commend representatives of respect for the military from all walks of life, so as to promote good relations between the military and the people and jointly build a warm and harmonious society

[1094] The procedure is as follows: Selection and commendation of representatives of good deeds: The Ministry of National Defense has formulated the "Implementation Guidelines for the Selection and Recommendation of Representatives of Good Deeds in the National Armed Forces". Every July, each grassroots unit will recommend candidates level by level. Those who meet the recommendation qualifications will be recommended to the National Good Deeds Commendation Preparatory Committee. The selected personnel will be commended by all sectors of the country in December each year

[1095] Selection and commendation of models of serving the people: According to the annual "Implementation Guidelines for the Selection and Commendation of Models of Serving the People in the Armed Forces", the National Defense Report of the Republic of China distinguishes between model units and model individuals of serving the people. Each grassroots unit recommends candidates level by level, and after approval by the Ministry of National Defense, each service (general) command commends them in conjunction with the September 3rd Military Day activities

[1096] Selection and Recognition of Models of Respect for the Military: To recognize the enthusiasm of all sectors of society in promoting the work of respecting and supporting the military, each year, the import and export associations, film and television associations, and other social organizations, as well as the military service stations under the Republic of China Military Friends Association, jointly recommend those who have made outstanding achievements in carrying out activities to respect and support the military. These individuals are then approved by the Ministry of National Defense as "Models of Respect for the Military" and publicly commended on September 3rd, Military Day. In 2002-2003, a total of 57 Models of Respect for the Military were commended

[1097] National Armed Forces Cultural Relics Exhibition To promote public understanding of the National Armed Forces, the Ministry of National Defense has redesigned the exhibits of the "National Armed Forces History Museum" to highlight the history of the National Armed Forces and to promote the patriotic sentiments of our predecessors and martyrs who sacrificed their lives

[1098] Regular exhibitions include "From the Founding of the Whampoa Military Academy to the Northern Expedition and Unification," a display of historical artifacts showcasing the glorious history of the National Revolutionary Army, "Resistance Against Japan," "The Suppression of the Rebellion and the Taiwan Strait Wars," "The Modernization of the National Revolutionary Army," an "Armaments Room," and a "Rank Exhibition Room." Thematic exhibitions are held for specific holidays, celebrations, and important exercises. From July 2002 to November 2004, the following exhibitions were held: "National Revolutionary Army Emblem Exhibition," "National Revolutionary Army War Painting Exhibition," "Special Exhibition on the History of the National Revolutionary Army's Seagull Unit," "Special Exhibition on the History of the National Revolutionary Army's Dunmu Detachment," and "80th Anniversary of the Founding of the Whampoa Military Academy." "Anniversary Historical Exhibition" and "National Armed Forces Arts and Literature Awards Ceremony Exhibition

[1099] II. Policy Direction (I) Strengthen the recognition of exemplary figures. The various exemplary representatives selected by the National Army will be included in the teaching materials of the annual public service education and cadre training, or widely reported by the media, to establish a model of loving and helping the people, promote positive interaction, and unite the people

[1100] II) Expand the effectiveness of services for families of soldiers and officers. In order to expand the effectiveness of the family gatherings and services for families of soldiers and officers, we will combine existing publicity channels to strengthen publicity, so as to popularize the activities and increase the participation of soldiers and their families

[1101] The Ministry of National Defense's "National Defense Report" can be considered the most important statutory document of the Ministry. It is compiled and printed regularly in accordance with the law to keep the public informed about the current state of national defense. Therefore, each publication has received attention and importance from all sectors

[1102] To ensure rigor, thoroughness, and the inclusion of diverse suggestions, over thirty scholars and experts from outside the ministry were invited to serve as advisory committee members. Various staff units within the ministry, as well as professors and instructors from the National Defense University and the Political Warfare Academy, were appropriately organized to carry out the various tasks

[1103] In terms of compilation methods, the headquarters established an editorial committee and invited advisory committee members to provide suggestions at any time for compilation reference; the various units of the headquarters collected and wrote the initial drafts, and adopted a joint review method to complete the revision and publication of the entire book

[1104] By June of this year, all the initial drafts had been largely completed. However, in order to achieve coordination and consistency with the National Security Strategy of the National Security Council and to demonstrate the Ministry of National Defense's forward-looking governance philosophy, policy planning, and the spirit of government consistency, the second phase of drafting began in July

[1105] Minister Lee Chieh convened a policy guidance group to expand the scope of recommendations by hiring more advisory committee members. Deputy Minister Tsai Ming-hsien oversaw the entire process, coordinating efforts from top to bottom and involving relevant government agencies, experts, and scholars to carry out a comprehensive revision

[1106] This compilation features: a two-stage process to enrich the content, improve the overall quality, and enhance its reference value; close collaboration with various ministries to demonstrate national security consensus and the spirit of the government's overall policy; detailed explanations of current national defense policies and military strategies, increasing transparency; and a new horizontal layout for a more dynamic and readable format. The publication of this book is a result of the collective wisdom of all participating units and colleagues, and the professional guidance of experts and scholars. We believe its detailed content will provide readers with valuable insights into national defense and research references. We also hope to continue to achieve a national consensus on national defense through continued transparency

[1107] Finally, the selfless dedication and contributions of all officers and soldiers of the National Armed Forces are truly the essence of this book; the compilation and printing of future "National Defense Reports" will continue to be carried out with the support of the people for the National Armed Forces
